Third-party source license and notice texts for the experimental mobile candidate

Exact components and versions are listed in android/docs/THIRD_PARTY_COMPONENTS.csv. Go versions come from candidate binary build info; Rust crates come from the Android ARM64 Cargo target graph. Java libraries are covered by android-library-LICENSES.txt and zxing-core-3.5.3-NOTICE.txt. Source license and notice file bytes below are copied verbatim from exact module/crate source archives; section headings are added for attribution. The supplied dirty Core binary and bundled assets still require final review.
